fragment.wave_count != 1\n            or len(fragment.waves) != 1\n            or fragment.planned_stage_ids != (stage_id,)\n        ):\n            raise ConnectedDomainPipelineManifestError(\n previous_status,\n            )\n\n        if disposition == \"completed\":\n            if stage_id in failed:\n                raise RuntimeCoordinationGatewayError(\n                    \"Completed dependency_job_ids_by_stage=dependency_job_ids_by_stage,\n        execution_semantics=execution_semantics,\n        submitter=submitter,\n        correlation_registry=correlation_registry,\n    )\n reconciled_stage_ids = []\n    created_stage_ids = []\n    for mapping in prepared:\n        key = execution_keys[mapping.stage_id]\n        matches = _e2_existing_for_key(jobs=loaded, key=key)\n        if len(matches) > 1:\n            raise _wave_error(\"Multiple persisted jobs share one coordination execution key.\",\n                              code=\"idempotency_reconciliation_conflict\", submit_runtime_job_mapping(\n                    mapping=mapping,\n                    submitter=lambda **_kwargs: reused,\n                    correlation_registry=effective_registry,\n                )\n                reconciled_stage_ids.append(mapping.stage_id)\n            else:\n                def keyed_submitter(\n                    _submitter=submitter,\n                    _key=key,\n                    **kwargs,\n                ):\n                    arguments = dict(kwargs)\n                    arguments[\"idempotency_key\"] = _key\n                    return _submitter(**arguments)\n\n                result = submit_runtime_job_mapping(\n                    mapping=mapping,\n                    submitter=keyed_submitter,\n                    correlation_registry=effective_registry,\n                )\n                created_stage_ids.append(mapping.stage_id)\n        except Exception as error:\n            raise _wave_error(\n                f\"Idempotent wave submission failed at stage {mapping.stage_id!r}: {type(error).__name__}: {error}\",\n                code=\"wave_submission_failure\", violations=(mapping.stage_id,), workflow_id=workflow_id,\n                wave_index=wave_index, failed_stage_id=mapping.stage_id,\n                submitted_results=tuple(submitted),\n            ) from error\n        result = key,\n            },\n        )\n\n        submitted.append(result)\n\n    submissions = tuple(submitted)\n    evidence = {\n        \"preflight_complete\": True,\n        \"all_registrations_resolved\": True,\n        \"canonical_single_mapping_gateway\": \"submit_runtime_job_mapping\",\n        \"canonical_job_store\": \"orchestration.job_store.load_jobs\",\n        \"idempotency_authority\": \"universal_job_idempotency_key\",\n        \"execution_identity_owner\": \"universal_coordination_runtime_execution_gateway\",\n        \"phase_5_2_mapping_unchanged\": True,\n        \"reconciliation_complete\": True,\n        \"reconciled_stage_ids\": tuple(reconciled_stage_ids),\n        \"created_stage_ids\": tuple(created_stage_ids),\n        \"partial_failure_evidence_retained\": True,\n        \"handler_dispatched\": False,\n    }\n    return RuntimeExecutionWaveGatewayResult(\n        contract_version=RUNTIME_EXECUTION_WAVE_GATEWAY_CONTRACT_VERSION,\n        gateway_version=RUNTIME_EXECUTION_WAVE_GATEWAY_VERSION,\n        workflow_id=workflow_id, wave_index=wave_index, execution_semantics=semantics,\n        stage_ids=stage_ids, submission_count=len(submissions),\n        created_count=sum(1 for item in submissions if item.created),\n        reused_existing_count=sum(1 for item in submissions if item.reused_existing_job),\n        queued_count=sum(1 for item in submissions if item.queued),\n        job_ids=tuple(item.job_id for item in submissions),\n        dependency_job_ids_by_stage=effective_dependencies,\n        mappings=tuple(prepared), submissions=submissions, evidence=evidence,\n    )"

          "source": "def submit_runtime_job_mapping(\n    *,\n    mapping: RuntimeJobMapping,\n    submitter: Callable[\n        ...,\n        Mapping[str, Any],\n    ] = submit_universal_job,\n               "source": "def save_workflow(\n        self,\n        workflow: UniversalWorkflow,\n        *,\n        expected_current_fingerprint: Optional[str] = None,\n    ) -> WorkflowPersistenceEvidence:\n        if not isinstance(\n            workflow,\n            UniversalWorkflow,\n        ):\n            raise TypeError(\n                \"workflow must be a UniversalWorkflow.\"\n            )\n\n        validation = workflow.validate()\n\n        if not validation.is_valid:\n            raise WorkflowPersistenceIntegrityError(\n                \"Workflow contract validation failed: \"\n                + \"; \".join(validation.violations)\n            )\n\n        workflow_id = _required_text(\n            workflow.workflow_id,\n            field_name=\"workflow_id\",\n        )\n\n        target = self.workflow_path(\n            workflow_id\n        )\n\n        with self._lock:\n            if expected_current_fingerprint is not              ),\n                )\n\n                if not target.is_file():\n                    raise WorkflowPersistenceConflictError(\n                        \"Expected an existing workflow, \"\n                        \"but no persisted workflow exists.\"\n                    )\n\n                current = self.load_workflow_record(\n                    workflow_id\n                )\n\n identity_fingerprint\n                ),\n                \"persisted_at\": persisted_at,\n                \"workflow\": workflow_payload,\n            }\n\n            document_fingerprint =             "source": "def build_active_target_set(\n    *,\n    workspace_id: str,\n    live_domain_payload: Mapping[str, Any] | None = None,\n) -> ActiveTargetSetResult:\n    workspace = _clean_text(workspace_id)\n\n    if not workspace:\n        raise ValueError(\n            \"workspace_id is required\"\n        )\n\n    sources = [\n        (\n            \"live_domain\",\n            \"live_domain_target_pool\",\n            live_domain_payload,\n        ),\n    ]\n\n    source_counts = {\n        target_type: len(\n            _payload_items(payload)\n        )\n        for target_type, _, payload in sources\n    }\n\n    rejected_examples: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []\n    rejection_reason_counts: Dict[str, int] = {}\n\n    normalized: List[ActiveTargetRecord] = []\n\n    for target_type, source_pool, payload in sources:\n        normalized.extend(\n            _normalize_source(\n                workspace_id=workspace,\n                target_type=target_type,\n                source_pool=source_pool,\n                payload=payload,\n                rejected_examples=rejected_examples,\n                rejection_reason_counts=(\n                    rejection_reason_counts\n                ),\n            )\n        )\n\n    deduplicated: Dict[\n        tuple[str, str],\n        ActiveTargetRecord,\n    ] = {}\n\n    for record in normalized:\n        key = (\n            record.target_type,\n            record.canonical_url,\n        )\n\n        if key in deduplicated:\n            reason = \"duplicate_target\"\n            rejection_reason_counts[reason] = (\n                rejection_reason_counts.get(reason, 0)\n                + 1\n            )\n            continue\n\n        deduplicated[key] = record\n\n    items = sorted(\n        deduplicated.values(),\n        key=lambda item: (\n            item.target_type,\n            item.canonical_url,\n            item.target_id,\n        ),\n    )\n\n    active_live_domain_urls = sorted(\n        {\n            item.canonical_url\n            for item in items\n            if item.target_type == \"live_domain\"\n        }\n    )\n\n    active_counts = {\n        \"total\": len(items),\n        \"live_domain\": len(\n            active_live_domain_urls\n        ),\n    }\n\n    return ActiveTargetSetResult(\n        schema_version=SCHEMA_VERSION,\n        workspace_id=workspace,\n        source_counts=source_counts,\n        active_counts=active_counts,\n        active_live_domain_urls=(\n            active_live_domain_urls\n        ),\n        items=items,\n        rejected_count=sum(\n            rejection_reason_counts.values()\n        ),\n        rejection_reason_counts=(\n            rejection_reason_counts\n        ),\n        rejected_examples=rejected_examples,\n    )"
          }

            "source": "def build_live_domain_target_pool(\n    site_pages: Iterable[Any],\n    *,\n    workspace_id: str,\n    domain: str,\n) -> LiveDomainTargetPoolResult:\n    normalized_workspace_id = str(\n        workspace_id\n        or \"\"\n    ).strip()\n\n    normalized_domain = _normalize_domain(\n        domain\n    )\n\n    if not normalized_workspace_id:\n        raise ValueError(\n            \"workspace_id is required\"\n        )\n\n    if not normalized_domain:\n        raise ValueError(\n            \"domain is required\"\n        )\n\n    records = list(site_pages or [])\n\n    result = LiveDomainTargetPoolResult(\n        workspace_id=normalized_workspace_id,\n        domain=normalized_domain,\n        input_count=len(records),\n    )\n\n    seen_canonical_urls: set[str] = set()\n    seen_target_ids: set[str] = set()\n\n    def reject(reason: str) -> None:\n        result.rejected_count += 1\n        result.rejection_reason_counts[reason] = (\n            result.rejection_reason_counts.get(\n                reason,\n                0,\n            )\n            + 1\n        )\n\n    for site_page in records:\n        url = str(\n            _read_value(\n                site_page,\n                \"url\",\n                \"\",\n            )\n            or \"\"\n        ).strip()\n\n        canonical_url = str(\n            _read_value(\n                site_page,\n                \"canonical_url\",\n                \"\",\n            )\n            or \"\"\n        ).strip()\n\n        record_workspace_id = str(\n            _read_value(\n                site_page,\n                \"workspace_id\",\n                \"\",\n            )\n            or \"\"\n        ).strip()\n\n        record_domain = _normalize_domain(\n            _read_value(\n                site_page,\n                \"domain\",\n                \"\",\n            )\n        )\n\n        site_page_status = str(\n            _read_value(\n                site_page,\n                \"status\",\n                \"\",\n            )\n            or \"\"\n        ).strip().lower()\n\n        confidence = str(\n            _read_value(\n                site_page,\n                \"cleaner_confidence\",\n                \"\",\n            )\n            or \"\"\n        ).strip().lower()\n\n        if not url:\n            reject(\"missing_url\")\n            continue\n\n        if not canonical_url:\n            reject(\"missing_canonical_url\")\n            continue\n\n        parsed = urlparse(url)\n\n        if not parsed.scheme or not parsed.hostname:\n            reject(\"invalid_url\")\n            continue\n\n        if record_workspace_id != normalized_workspace_id:\n            reject(\"workspace_mismatch\")\n            continue\n\n        if record_domain != normalized_domain:\n            reject(\"domain_mismatch\")\n            continue\n\n        if site_page_status != SITE_PAGE_STATUS_READY:\n            reject(\"site_page_not_ready\")\n            continue\n\n        if confidence not in VALID_CLEANER_CONFIDENCE:\n            reject(\"invalid_cleaner_confidence\")\n            continue\n\n        if canonical_url in seen_canonical_urls:\n            reject(\"duplicate_canonical_url\")\n            continue\n\n        target_id = _target_id(\n            normalized_workspace_id,\n            canonical_url,\n        )\n\n        if target_id in seen_target_ids:\n            reject(\"duplicate_target_id\")\n            continue\n\n        seen_canonical_urls.add(\n            canonical_url\n        )\n        seen_target_ids.add(\n            target_id\n        )\n\n        result.targets.append(\n            LiveDomainTargetRecord(\n                target_id=target_id,\n                url=url,\n                canonical_url=canonical_url,\n                domain=normalized_domain,\n                workspace_id=normalized_workspace_id,\n                source_type=SOURCE_TYPE_LIVE_DOMAIN,\n                site_page_status=site_page_status,\n                cleaner_confidence=confidence,\n                target_status=TARGET_STATUS_AVAILABLE,\n                created_at=result.generated_at,\n            )\n        )\n\n    result.created_count = len(\n        result.targets\n    )\n\n    if (\n        result.created_count\n        + result.rejected_count\n        != result.input_count\n    ):\n        raise RuntimeError(\n            \"Live Domain Target Pool count \"\n            \"reconciliation failed\"\n        )\n\n    return result"
